[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

... (showing lines 1-147 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='starting_point.v', offset=147, limit=400)
  -> read_file(filename='tb.sv', offset=147, limit=30)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 3/60]
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

[Step 3/60]
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);
          else
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+1], coeffs[b*64+r*8+3],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+5], coeffs[b*64+r*8+7]);
        end
      end
      // inter-block gap: >= 2 idle cycles (serializer IDLE+SETUP), varied a bit
      repeat (2 + (b % 3)) drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    end
    drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    repeat (400) @(negedge clk);  // flush x + transpose + y pipeline

    if (out_cnt != num_blocks * 64) begin
      $display("TB_ERROR missing outputs: got %0d, expected %0d", out_cnt, num_blocks * 64);
      total_errors = total_errors + 1;
    end
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)

    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)

        mul0_b <= C2_16;

    3'd1:

Instead:\n   restructure so that each of the 4 read lanes uses its own bank of 8x32b\n   (bank = row of destination), i.e. store the block in 8 rows x 8 cols.\n   Write of row r goes to all... hmm — write addr = {buf,row}: data0..3 lanes\n   on a beat all belong to the SAME input row, and the beat writes 4 different\n   columns of that row. So bank r (row r) needs 4 write ports (one per lane,\n   distinct columns) and, on read, lane j needs column c=col_seq[k] of row\n   row_seq[k] -> reads column col of bank row -> out of ONE bank per lane beat.\n   So implement bank = array of 8 regs [31:0] x 8 columns with 4 dedicated\n   write ports (lane0..3) and 1 read port (8:1 mux of 32b).\n   Total FFs same (2048) but decode is 3-bit row select -> 8 rows of 8 regs\n   (8 write-enable groups of 4 lanes each) + 8:1 mux per lane. Much less mux\n   logic: instead of 4 x (32:1 x32b shared 5-bit decode across 4 RAMs).\n\n2. 1-D passes: mul_b is a constant per beat -> make multiplier constant-aware\n   (mul with muxed shift-add). Try: replace 32x32 signed mult with constant\n   multipliers using shift-add on 12-bit constants (C1..C7) and the *181/256.\n   Big area saving (10 x 32x32 mults -> 5 per pass; constant -> ~5 adders).\n\n3. Delay: reported delay 2222 ps is the RAM decode path; after fix, the mult\n   path (~? ps) dominates. Can pipeline by one more cycle if needed (gap\n   tolerant? need care with row pass block_out/valid timing and streamer\n   overlap — maybe safe since downstream is elastic double buffer... the\n   transpose FSM needs ready=1; deeper pipeline adds latency but the overlap\n   means throughput must be >= 1 beat/cycle on average; fine).\n\n4. Try target_delay sweep (500 -> 1000/1500) after structural fixes.\n\nCost model: ADP = area * delay. Current: area 4274, delay 2222.\nMultiplier area is probably ~40% of total. 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;\n    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;\n    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;\n    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t1;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t2;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t3;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t4;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\n\n// (x*181)/256 with signed truncation toward zero:\n//   pos: (x*181 + 256) >> 8 ; neg: (x*181 - 255) >> 8  (arithmetic shift)\nfunction signed [31:0] m181_div256;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg   [63:0] p64;\n    reg signed [31:0] end\nendfunction\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 32'b0; o_s6 <= 32'b0; o_s7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t0 <= 32'b0; o_t1 <= 32'b0; o_t2 <= 32'b0; o_t3 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t4 <= 32'b0; o_t5 o_t0 - o_t3; // t3\n        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4\n m181_div256(o_t6_5); // 1/sqrt(2)\n        o_s6 <= m181_div256(o_t5_6);\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;\n    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 32'b0; block_out[1] <= 32'b0; block_out[2] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[3] <= 32'b0; block_out[4] <= 32'b0; block_out[5] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[6] <= 32'b0; block_out[7] <= 32'b0; block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// Double-buffered corner turn (transpose), functionally identical to\n// jpeg_idct_transpose but with the four 32x32 dual-port RAMs replaced\n// by one shared bank structure (same storage, simpler decode).\n//=====================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0] block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\n\nwire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};\n\n// Four independent write lanes (one per beat data lane).\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\nwire [31:0] outport_data0_w;\nwire [31:0] outport_data1_w;\nwire [31:0] outport_data2_w;\nwire [31:0] outport_data3_w;\n\n// Storage: 4 buffers x 8 rows x 8 cols x 32b = 2048 FFs.\n// Read mux: row (5:3) select then col (2:0) select.\nreg [31:0] bufs [0:63];\nreg [31:0] rd0_q;\nreg [31:0] rd1_q;\nreg [31:0] rd2_q;\nreg [31:0] rd3_q;\n\nwire [2:0] wr_row_w = inport_idx_i[3+3:3];\nwire [2:0] wr_col_w = inport_idx_i[2:0];\nwire [5:0] rd_row_w = {block_rd_q, rd_addr_q[3:1]};\nwire [2:0] rd_col_w = rd_addr_q[2:0];\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr0_w) bufs[{block_wr_q, wr_row_w, wr_col_w}] <= inport_data_i;\n    if (wr1_w) bufs[{block_wr_q, wr_row_w, 3'b100 + wr_col_w}] <= inport_data_i;\n    if (wr2_w) bufs[{block_wr_q, wr_row_w, 3'b000 + wr_col_w}] <= inport_data_i;\n    if (wr3_w) bufs[{block_wr_q, wr_row_w, 3'b100 + wr_col_w}] <= inport_data_i;\n\n    rd0_q <= bufs[{rd_row_w, rd_col_w}];\n    rd1_q <= bufs[{rd_row_w, 3'b100 + rd_col_w}];\n    rd2_q <= bufs[{rd_row_w, 3'b000 + rd_col_w}];\n    rd3_q <= bufs[{rd_row_w, 3'b100 + rd_col_w}];\nend\n\n// (unused placeholders to keep port names)\nwire [31:0] unused0 = 32'b0;\n\nassign outport_data0_o = rd0_q;\nassign outport_data1_o = rd1_q;\nassign outport_data2_o = rd2_q;\nassign outport_data3_o = rd3_q;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Input Buffer\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// FSM\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n    begin\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    end\n (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\nbegin\n    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    3'd2: ;\n    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    endcase\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// Column pass (OUT_SHIFT = 15)\n//=====================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n#(\n     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n   mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= 4'd3;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= 4'd4;\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n mul4_b <= 4'd4;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\nend\n\n// Constant multiplier: b in {1..7} maps to C1..C7. Shift-add expansion.\nfunction signed [31:0] cmul;\n    input signed [31:0] a;\n    input        [ 3:0] b;\n    reg signed [31:0] s10 = a <<< 10;\n    reg signed [31:0] s11 = a <<< 11;\n    reg signed [31:0] s12 = a <<< 12;\n    reg signed [31:0] acc;\n    begin\n        case (b)\n        4'd1: acc = s12 - ((a<<<6)+(a<<3)+(a<<2)+(a<<1)+a);\n        4'd2: acc = s12 - ((a<<8)+(a<<5)+(a<<4)+(a<<3));\n        4'd3: acc = s12 - ((a<<9)+(a<<7)+(a<<5)+(a<<4)+(a<<1));\n        4'd4: acc = s12 - ((a<<10)+(a<<7)+(a<<5)+(a<<4));\n        4'd5: acc = s11 + ((a<<7)+(a<<6)+(a<<5)+(a<<2));\n        4'd6: acc = s11 - ((a<<8)+(a<<7)+(a<<6)+(a<<5)+a);\n        default: acc = s10 - ((a<<7)+(a<<6)+(a<<5)+a);\n        endcase\n        cmul = acc;\n    end\nendfunction\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul1_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul2_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul3_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul4_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= 32'b0; o_s6 <= 32'b0; o_s7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t0 <= 32'b0; o_t1 <= 32'b0; o_t2 <= 32'b0; o_t3 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t4 <= 32'b0; o_t5 <= 32'b0; o_t6 <= 32'b0; o_t7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0; o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;\n        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;\n        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0\n        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; [ASSISTANT]
Found the bugs by tracing the transpose addressing (slot `{row, j}`, lane = `row[2:1]`, word = `{row[0], j}` — my storage mapping is equivalent; blocks 0-2 pass so the framework works). The remaining failures are in my 1-D pass: (1) the y-pass zero-extends instead of using full 32-bit inputs, (2) my 16-bit operand truncation drops bit 16 of `i0` (=c0+c4, 17-bit), (3) my C5 shift-add decomposition was wrong (2148≠2276). Let me rewrite with a parameterized 1-D core: x-pass operands 17-bit/29-bit exact products; y-pass operands 21-bit (inputs provably fit 20 bits signed) with 32-bit wrap products exactly matching the original.

31\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\nfunction signed [MW-1:0] cmul;\n    input signed [OPW-1:0] a;\n    input         [ 2:0] b;\n    reg signed [28:0] n0, n1, + q2; end\n            3'd3: begin q1 = n12 - n9;  q2 = n7 + n5;  q3 = n4 + n1;\n                        acc29 = (q1 - q2) - q3; end\n            3'd4: begin q1 = n12 - n10; q2 = n7 + n5;  acc29 = (q1 - q2) - n4; end\n            3'd5: begin q1 = n11 + n7;  q2 = n6 + n5;  acc29 = (q1 + q2) + n2; end\n            3'd6: begin q1 = n11 - n9;  q2 = n5 - n0;  acc29 = q1 + q2; end\n            default: 3'd2: begin r1 = w12 - w8;  r2 = w3 - w6;  acc32 = r1 + r2; end\n            3'd3: begin r1 = w12 - w9;  r2 = w7 + w5;  r3 = w4 + w1;\n                        acc32 = (r1 - r2) - r3; end\n w5 - a;   acc32 = r1 + r2; end\n            default: begin r1 = w10 - w8; r2 = w5 - a; acc32 = r1 + r2; end\n            endcase\n            cmul = acc32;\n        end\n    end\nendfunction\n\n// m181_div256;\n    input signed [OW-1:0] x;\n    reg signed [31:0] p1, p2, p;\n    begin\n        p1 = (x <<< 7) + (x <<< 5);   // x*160\n        p2 = (x <<< 4) + (x <<< 2);   // x*20\n        p  = (p1 [ASSISTANT]
The 17-bit combining registers were far too narrow — the real intermediate values reach ~2^29 (the constants already include the ×4096 scaling). Let me fix the widths: OW=32 for both passes (exact match to original wrap behavior), keep the savings in the constant multipliers (17/21-bit operands), the 20-bit transpose storage, and the narrow block_out:
  -> create_file(content="//=====================================================================\n// Optimized full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg bit-exact behavior).\n//\n//   jpeg_idct_x (row pass, + r2; end\n        3'd3: begin r1 = s12 - s9;  r2 = s7 + s5;  r3 = s4 + s1;\n                    cmul = (r1 - r2) - r3; end\n        3'd4: begin r1 = s12 - s10; r2 = s7 + s5;  cmul = (r1 - r2) - s4; end\n        3'd5: begin r1 = s11 + s7;  r2 = s6 + s5;  cmul = (r1 + r2) + s2; end\n        3'd6: begin r1 = s11 - s9;  r2 = s5 - a32; cmul = r1 + r2; end\n        default: begin r1 = s10 - s8; r2 = s5 - a32; cmul = r1 + r2; end\n        endcase\n    end\nendfunction\n\n// (x*181)/256 with signed division truncating toward zero.\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 exactly as the original; then\n//   p >= 0 : p >>> 8 ;  p < 0 : (p + 255) >>> 8.\nfunction signed [31:0] m181_div256;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg signed [31:0] p1, p2, p;\n    begin\n        p1 = (x <<< 7) + (x <<< 5);   // x*160\n // block_out[7]\n        block_out[3]   <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4]   <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nwire [BOW-1:0] out_sel_u = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = out_sel_u;\n// Column pass emits column-major (slot = {column, row}); the streamed\n// idx must label the row-major pixel position {slot[2:0], slot[5:3]}.\nassign outport_idx_o   = (PERM_IDX != 0) ? {ptr_q[2:0], ptr_q[5:3]} : ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// Double-buffered corner turn.\n//\n// The row pass emits slot {row[2:0], j[2:0]}; storage word address is\n// {buffer, lane, offset} with lane = row[2:1] and offset = {row[0], j}\n// — exactly the original's four 32x32 RAMs (lane selects the RAM,\n// offset the word).  The column pass reads, per beat, all four lanes\n// at one offset, so each read lane is a 32:1 mux of 20-bit words.\n//=====================================================================\nmodule  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]   outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]   outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0] endmodule

//=====================================================================
// Shared 1-D factorized (AAN-style) IDCT pass.
//   IW_16 = 1 : 16-bit signed input lanes (row pass)
//   IW_16 = 0 : 32-bit lanes holding 20-bit signed values (column pass)
//   OPW  : multiplier operand register width (17 = row, 21 = col)
//   BOW  : block_out register width
//=====================================================================
module jpeg_idct_1d
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT = 11,
     parameter IW_16     = 1,
     parameter OPW       = 17,
     parameter BOW       = 20,
     parameter PERM_IDX  = 0
)
(
     input            clk
    ,input            rst
    ,input            img_start_i
    ,input            inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 19:0]   inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 19:0]   inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 19:0]   inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 19:0]   inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]   inport_idx_i
    ,output           outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 19:0]   outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]   outport_idx_o
);

// Operand registers are sign-extended copies of the inputs:
// row pass:  16-bit coefficients -> 17-bit regs
// col pass:  20-bit values       -> 21-bit regs
wire [OPW-1:0] a0 = (IW_16 != 0) ? {inport_data0_i[15], inport_data0_i[15:0]}
                                 : {inport_data0_i[19], inport_data0_i[19:0]};
wire [OPW-1:0] a2 = (IW_16 != 0) ? {inport_data1_i[15], inport_data1_i[15:0]}
                                 : {inport_data1_i[19], inport_data1_i[19:0]};
wire [OPW-1:0] a4 = (IW_16 != 0) ? {inport_data2_i[15], inport_data2_i[15:0]}
                                 : {inport_data2_i[19], inport_data2_i[19:0]};
wire [OPW-1:0] a6 = (IW_16 != 0) ? {inport_data3_i[15], inport_data3_i[15:0]}
                                 : {inport_data3_i[19], inport_data3_i[19:0]};

//---------------------------------------------------------------------
// Constant multiplier: b in {1..7} selects C1..C7.
// Evaluated in 32-bit wrap-around context after sign-extending the
// operand, exactly equal to (a * C) mod 2^32.
//   C1 = 4017 = 4096 - 64 - 15
//   C2 = 3784 = 4096 - 256 - 56
//   C3 = 3406 = 4096 - 512 - 160 - 18
//   C4 = 2896 = 4096 - 1024 - 160 - 16
//   C5 = 2276 = 2048 + 128 + 100
//   C6 = 1567 = 2048 - 512 + 31
//   C7 =  799 = 1024 - 256 + 31
//---------------------------------------------------------------------
function signed [31:0] cmul;
    input signed [OPW-1:0] a;
    input         [ 2:0] b;
    reg signed [31:0] a32;
    reg signed [31:0] s1, s2, s3, s4, s5, s6, s7, s8, s9, s10, s11, s12;
    reg signed [31:0] r1, r2, r3;
    begin
        a32 = a;
        s1  = a32 <<< 1;
        s2  = a32 <<< 2;
        s3  = a32 <<< 3;
        s4  = a32 <<< 4;
        s5  = a32 <<< 5;
        s6  = a32 <<< 6;
        s7  = a32 <<< 7;
        s8  = a32 <<< 8;
        s9  = a32 <<< 9;
        s10 = a32 <<< 10;
        s11 = a32 <<< 11;
        s12 = a32 <<< 12;
        case (b)
        3'd1: begin r1 = s12 - s6;  r2 = a32 - s4;  cmul = r1 + r2; end
        3'd2: begin r1 = s12 - s8;  r2 = s3 - s6;  cmul = r1 + r2; end
        3'd3: begin r1 = s12 - s9;  r2 = s7 + s5;  r3 = s4 + s1;
                    cmul = (r1 - r2) - r3; end
        3'd4: begin r1 = s12 - s10; r2 = s7 + s5;  cmul = (r1 - r2) - s4; end
        3'd5: begin r1 = s11 + s7;  r2 = s6 + s5;  cmul = (r1 + r2) + s2; end
        3'd6: begin r1 = s11 - s9;  r2 = s5 - a32; cmul = r1 + r2; end
        default: begin r1 = s10 - s8; r2 = s5 - a32; cmul = r1 + r2; end
        endcase
    end
endfunction

// (x*181)/256 with signed division truncating toward zero.
// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 exactly as the original; then
//   p >= 0 : p >>> 8 ;  p < 0 : (p + 255) >>> 8.
function signed [31:0] m181_div256;
    input signed [31:0] x;
    reg signed [31:0] p1, p2, p;
    begin
        p1 = (x <<< 7) + (x <<< 5);   // x*160
        p2 = (x <<< 4) + (x <<< 2);   // x*20
        p  = (p1 + p2) + x;           // x*181 (mod 2^32)
        m181_div256 = (p[31] ? (p + 32'sd255) : p) >>> 8;
    end
endfunction

//---------------------------------------------------------------------
// Operand/constant registers (5 time-shared multipliers)
//---------------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [OPW-1:0] i0;
reg signed [OPW-1:0] mul0_a;
reg         [ 2:0]   mul0_b;
reg signed [OPW-1:0] mul1_a;
reg         [ 2:0]   mul1_b;
reg signed [OPW-1:0] mul2_a;
reg         [ 2:0]   mul2_b;
reg signed [OPW-1:0] mul3_a;
reg         [ 2:0]   mul3_b;
reg signed [OPW-1:0] mul4_a;
reg         [ 2:0]   mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk)
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= {OPW{1'b0}};
    mul0_a <= {OPW{1'b0}}; mul0_b <= 3'b0;
    mul1_a <= {OPW{1'b0}}; mul1_b <= 3'b0;
    mul2_a <= {OPW{1'b0}}; mul2_b <= 3'b0;
    mul3_a <= {OPW{1'b0}}; mul3_b <= 3'b0;
    mul4_a <= {OPW{1'b0}}; mul4_b <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= a0 + a4;
        mul0_a <= a2; mul0_b <= 3'd2;
        mul1_a <= a6; mul1_b <= 3'd6;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= a0; mul0_b <= 3'd1;
        mul1_a <= a6; mul1_b <= 3'd7;
        mul2_a <= a4; mul2_b <= 3'd5;
        mul3_a <= a2; mul3_b <= 3'd3;
        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_b <= 3'd4;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= a0 - a4;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= a0; mul0_b <= 3'd7;
        mul1_a <= a6; mul1_b <= 3'd1;
        mul2_a <= a4; mul2_b <= 3'd3;
        mul3_a <= a2; mul3_b <= 3'd5;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= a0; mul0_b <= 3'd7;
        mul1_a <= a6; mul1_b <= 3'd1;
        mul2_a <= a4; mul2_b <= 3'd3;
        mul3_a <= a2; mul3_b <= 3'd5;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= a2; mul0_b <= 3'd6;
        mul1_a <= a6; mul1_b <= 3'd2;
        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_b <= 3'd4;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

[ASSISTANT]
**PASS!** design5 is bit-exact (0/20672 failures). But cost went UP (11.03M vs 9.50M): area dropped (4274→4077) but delay rose (2222→2705 ps). The critical path is still the transpose read: `rd_addr_q` → decode → 32:1 mux → `rd_q`. My single 128-entry array makes Yosys build a 128:1 mux per read port (worse than 4 separate 32-word RAMs).

Key insight for the delay: the read address is `{buf(1), word(4)}` — I can split the mux into two stages: two 16:1 muxes (one per buffer) → **register** → 2:1 by `block_rd_q`. This halves the mux depth. Let me restructure the transpose with per-lane, per-buffer arrays (8 arrays × 16 words):
  -> create_file(content="// Optimized full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg bit-exact).\n// x-pass (OUT_SHIFT=11) -> double-buffered corner turn -> y-pass (OUT_SHIFT=15).\n//\n// Savings vs. original:\n//  * 16-bit signed;\n//    (c0+c4)/(c0-c4) fit in [-2^16,2^16]).\n//  * Row-pass outputs bounded by 2^19 -> 20-bit corner-turn storage\n//    (all internal sums stay below 2^30, so no wrap).\n//  * 16-word arrays (lane x buffer), two-stage read mux\n//    (16:1 registered, then 2:1 by buffer) for short logic depth.\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n [ASSISTANT]
**PASS!** design6: ADP 8.04M (area 4097, delay 1963 ps). Still transpose-read dominated (rd_addr → 16:1 mux → rq). 

Now a much better transpose read structure. I derived the exact read address sequence: `addr(i) = g(i) + 8·b(k(i))` where `g = i[5:3]` (group/column, stable 8 cycles) and `b = k[0]^k[2]` (fast bit) — so each lane reads only **2 distinct words per group**. I can prefetch both words with 8:1 muxes whose select is the *next* group (registered-ish, stable for 8 cycles), then a tiny 2:1 by `b`. This deletes `rd_addr_q` entirely and cuts the read path to FF→8:1→FF→2:1→FF:
  -> create_file(content="// Optimized full 8x8 2-D IDCT (core_jpeg bit-exact).\n// x-pass (OUT_SHIFT=11) -> double-buffered corner turn -> y-pass (OUT_SHIFT=15).\n//\n// Savings vs. original:\n//  * Constant multipliers (C1..C7) as balanced shift/add trees (32-bit wrap\n//    context == (a*C) mod 2^32 of the original 32x32 signed multiply).\n//  * Row-pass operand registers 17-bit (coeffs are 16-bit signed;\n//    (c0+c4)/(c0-c4) fit in [-2^16,2^16]).\n//  * Row-pass outputs bounded by 2^19 -> 20-bit corner-turn storage\n//    (all internal sums stay below 2^30, so no wrap).\n//  * Column-pass operand registers 21-bit (20-bit stored values).\n//  * (x*181)/256: p = (x*181) mod 2^32; truncating signed div by 256:\n//    p>=0 : p>>>8 ; p<0 : (p+255)>>>8.\n//  * Corner turn read path: the original read address sequence is\n//    addr(i) = g(i) + 8*b(k(i)) with g = i[5:3] (stable for 8 beats) and\n//    b = k[0]^k[2]; each lane therefore only ever needs the two words\n//    {0,g} and {1,g} of its group.    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nwire        x_valid_w;\nwire [19:0] x_data_w;\nwire [ 5:0] x_idx_w;\n\njpeg_idct_1d\n#(\n     .OUT_SHIFT (11),\n     .IW_16     (1),\n     .OPW       (17),\n r2; end\n        3'd3: begin r1 = s12 - s9;  r2 = s7 + s5;  r3 = s4 + s1;\n                    cmul = (r1 - r2) - r3; end\n        3'd4: begin r1 = s12 - s10; r2 = s7 + s5;  cmul = (r1 - r2) - s4; end\n        3'd5: begin r1 = s11 + s7;  r2 = s6 + s5;  cmul = (r1 + r2) + s2; end\n        3'd6: begin r1 = s11 - s9;  r2 = s5 - a32; cmul = r1 + r2; end\n        default: begin r1 = s10 - s8; r2 = >>> 8 (plain truncating division, no bias)\n//   p <  0 : (p + 255) >>> 8\nfunction signed [31:0] m181_div256;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg signed [31:0] p1, p2, p;\n    begin\n        p1 = (x [ASSISTANT]
**PASS!** design7: **ADP 4.86M** (area 4212, delay 1154 ps) — halfway to baseline. Now the big area win: each shared `cmul` contains **all 7** case branches synthesized plus a 7:1 mux — ×5 multipliers = 35 trees per pass. Replacing with 7 *dedicated* fixed-constant shift-add trees (~19 small adders total, no result mux, no operand regs):

t2_c6 = (oc6 <<< 5) - oc6;\nassign q_c6  = t1_c6 + t2_c6;\n\nassign t1_c7 = (oc7 <<< 10) - (oc7 <<< 8);\nassign t2_c7 = (oc7 <<< 5) - oc7;\nassign q_c7  = t1_c7 + t2_c7;\n\n// product registers (2-stage, aligned with out_stg2_idx_q)\nreg signed [PW-1:0] pq1, pq2, pq3, pq4, pq5, pq6, pq7;\nreg signed [PW-1:0] p1,  p2,  p3,  p4,  p5,  p6,  p7;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    pq1 <= {PW{1'b0}}; pq2 <= {PW{1'b0}}; pq3 <= {PW{1'b0}};\n    pq4 <= {PW{1'b0}}; pq5 <= {PW{1'b0}}; pq6 <= {PW{1'b0}};\n    pq7 <= {PW{1'b0}};\n    p1 <= {PW{1'b0}}; p2 <= {PW{1'b0}}; p3 <= {PW{1'b0}};\n    p4 <= {PW{1'b0}}; p5 <= {PW{1'b0}}; p6 <= {PW{1'b0}};\n    p7 <= {PW{1'b0}};\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    pq1 <= q_c1; pq2 <= q_c2; pq3 <= q_c3;\n    pq4 <= q_c4; pq5 <= q_c5; pq6 <= q_c6; pq7 <= q_c7;\n    p1 <= pq1; p2 <= pq2; p3 <= pq3;\n    p4 <= pq4; p5 <= pq5; p6 <= pq6; p7 <= pq7;\nend\n\n// sign-extended products for the combining stage (32-bit context)\nwire signed [31:0] pc1 = {{(33-PW){p1[PW-1]}}, p1};\nwire signed [31:0] pc2 = {{(33-PW){p2[PW-1]}}, p2};\nwire signed [31:0] pc3 = {{(33-PW){p3[PW-1]}}, p3};\nwire signed [31:0] pc4 = {{(33-PW){p4[PW-1]}}, p4};\nwire signed [31:0] pc5 = {{(33-PW){p5[PW-1]}}, p5};\nwire signed [31:0] pc6 = {{(33-PW){p6[PW-1]}}, p6};\nwire signed [31:0] pc7 = {{(33-PW){p7[PW-1]}}, p7};\n\n// (x*181)/256 with signed division truncating toward zero.\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 (as the original); then\n//   p >= 0 : p >>> 8 (plain truncating division, no bias)\n//   p <  0 : (p + 255) >>> 8\nfunction signed [31:0] m181_div256;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    reg signed [31:0] p1x, p2x, p;\n    begin\n        p1x = (x <<< 7) + (x <<< 5);  // x*160\n        p2x = (x <<< 4) + (x <<< 2);  // x*20\n        p   = (p1x + p2x) + x;        // x*181 (mod 2^32)\n        m181_div256 = (p[31] ? (p + 32'sd255) : p) >>> 8;\n    end\nendfunction\n\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\n// i0 register (loaded on beats 0 and 2, used by the C4 tree)\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    i0 <= {OPW{1'b0}};\nelse if (idx_w == 3'd0)\n    i0 <= a0 + a4;\nelse if (idx_w == 3'd2)\n    i0 <= a0 - a4;\n\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipeline valid/idx tracking\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;\n    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;\n    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;\n    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Combining stage (beat-scheduled, 32-bit signed wrap-around)\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t1;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t2;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t3;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t4;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 32'b0; o_s6 <= 32'b0; o_s7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t0 <= 32'b0; o_t1 <= 32'b0; o_t2 <= 32'b0; o_t3 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t4 <= 32'b0; o_t5 <= 32'b0; o_t6 <= 32'b0; o_t7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0; o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        o_t3 <= pc2 + pc6; // s3 = a2*C2 + a6*C6\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        o_s7 <= pc1 + pc7;\n        o_s6 <= pc5 + pc3;\n        o_t0 <= pc4;       // s0\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0\n        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3\n        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        o_t4 <= (pc7 - pc1) + (pc3 - pc5);\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        o_t0 <= pc7 - pc1; // s4\n        o_s5 <= pc3 - pc5;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        o_t3 <= pc6 - pc2; // s2\n        o_t4 <= pc4;       // s1\n        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;\n        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n    end\n    3'd6:\n    begin\n        o_t1   <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n        o_t2   <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    default:\n    begin\n        o_s5 <= m181_div256(o_t6_5); // 1/sqrt(2)\n        o_s6 <= m181_div256(o_t5_6);\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;\n    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Output register file + streamer\n//---------------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [BOW-1:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [BOW-1:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= {BOW{1'b0}}; block_out[1] <= {BOW{1'b0}};\n    block_out[2] <= {BOW{1'b0}}; block_out[3] <= {BOW{1'b0}};\n    block_out[4] <= {BOW{1'b0}}; block_out[5] <= {BOW{1'b0}};\n    block_out[6] <= {BOW{1'b0}}; block_out[7] <= {BOW{1'b0}};\n    block_out_tmp <= {BOW{1'b0}};\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0]   <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp  <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]\n        block_out[3]   <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4]   <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nwire [BOW-1:0] out_sel_u = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = out_sel_u;\n// Column pass emits column-major (slot = {column, row}); the streamed\n// idx must label the row-major pixel position {slot[2:0], slot[5:3]}.\nassign outport_idx_o   = (PERM_IDX != 0) ? {ptr_q[2:0], ptr_q[5:3]} : ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//=====================================================================\n// Double-buffered corner turn.\n//\n// x-pass output slot {row[2:0], j[2:0]}: lane = row[2:1], word = {row[0], j}.\n// Storage: 8 arrays (buffer x lane) x 16 words x 20b.\n// Read: addr(i) = g(i) + 8*b(k(i)), g = i[5:3], b = k[0]^k[2] -> per lane\n// and group only words {0,g} and {1,g} are needed; both are prefetched\n// (select = next group id) and the per-beat word is a final 2:1 by b.\n//=====================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n ,input            outport_ready_i\n    ,output           outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]   outport_data0_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]   outport_data1_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]   outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]   outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]   outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\n// storage: mem{buf*4 + lane}[word], word = {row[0], j[2:0]}\nreg [19:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [19:0] mem1 [0:15];\nreg [19:0] mem2 [0:15];\nreg [19:0] mem3 [0:15];\nreg index within the group)\nwire b_w = rd_idx_q[0] ^ rd_idx_q[2];\n\nassign outport_data0_o = block_rd_q ? [ASSISTANT]
The dedicated-multiplier mapping has two bugs: products need **3** register stages (operand-mux → tree → 3 regs) to align with the combining stage, and I missed that the C2/C6 trees are also fed at input beat 2 (for combining beat 5). Fixing:
  -> edit_file(filename='design8.v', new_string="// per-constant operand select (2:1 muxes; unused beats don't care)\n// C2/C6 are consumed at combining beats 0 (from ib0 and ib2) and 5 (ib5).\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c2 = (idx_w == 3'd5) ? a6 : a2;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c6 = (idx_w == 3'd5) ? a2 : a6;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c1 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a0 : a6;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c7 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a6 : a0;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c5 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a4 : a2;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c3 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a2 : a4;", old_string="// per-constant operand select (2:1 muxes; unused beats don't care)\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c2 = (idx_w == 3'd0) ? a2 : a6;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c6 = (idx_w == 3'd0) ? a6 : a2;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c1 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a0 : a6;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c7 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a6 : a0;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c5 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a4 : a2;\nwire [OPW-1:0] op_c3 = (idx_w == 3'd1) ? a2 : a4;")

wire signed [31:0] pc1 = {{(33-PW){p1[PW-1]}}, p1};
wire signed [31:0] pc2 = {{(33-PW){p2[PW-1]}}, p2};
wire signed [31:0] pc3 = {{(33-PW){p3[PW-1]}}, p3};
wire signed [31:0] pc4 = {{(33-PW){p4[PW-1]}}, p4};
wire signed [31:0] pc5 = {{(33-PW){p5[PW-1]}}, p5};
wire signed [31:0] pc6 = {{(33-PW){p6[PW-1]}}, p6};
wire signed [31:0] pc7 = {{(33-PW){p7[PW-1]}}, p7};

// (x*181)/256 with signed division truncating toward zero.
// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 (as the original); then
//   p >= 0 : p >>> 8 (plain truncating division, no bias)
//   p <  0 : (p + 255) >>> 8
function signed [31:0] m181_div256;
    input signed [31:0] x;
    reg signed [31:0] p1x, p2x, p;
    begin
        p1x = (x <<< 7) + (x <<< 5);  // x*160
        p2x = (x <<< 4) + (x <<< 2);  // x*20
        p   = (p1x + p2x) + x;        // x*181 (mod 2^32)
        m181_div256 = (p[31] ? (p + 32'sd255) : p) >>> 8;
    end
endfunction

if (rst)
    i0 <= {OPW{1'b0}};

## Summary

**Final result: `design7.v` — PASS, ADP 4.86M** (area 4212 µm², delay 1154 ps) vs. baseline `design.v` (original core_jpeg composition) at 9.50M (area 4274, delay 2222 ps). ~2× improvement.

### 1. Approaches tried
- **`design.v`** (baseline, included the original RTL): ADP 9.50M, delay 2222 ps — critical path was the transpose RAM read decode (5-bit addr → 32-entry register decode → 32:1×32b mux).
- **`design5.v`** — shared parameterized 1-D pass (`jpeg_idct_1d`) with 5 time-shared constant shift-add multipliers (17/21-bit operand regs), 20-bit transpose storage, single 128-word array: PASS but ADP 11.0M — Yosys built a 128:1 mux per read (worse than 4×32:1).
- **`design6.v`** — transpose as 8 arrays of 16 words (buffer×lane) with two-stage read mux: **ADP 8.04M** (delay 1963).
- **`design7.v`** — algebraic read-path redesign (see below): **ADP 4.86M** ✅.
- **`design8.v`** — 7 dedicated constant multipliers replacing 5 time-shared ones: never reached correctness (beat-alignment bugs, ~19400/20672 failing at step limit; reverted).

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
- **Transpose read-path algebra** (biggest win): derived that the original's read address is `addr(i) = g(i) + 8·b(k(i))` with `g = i[5:3]` (stable 8 cycles) and `b = k[0]^k[2]`, so each lane needs only 2 words per group. Two 8:1 prefetch muxes (select = *next* group) + one 2:1 by `b` per lane → delay 1963→1154 ps.
- **Two-stage registered mux** (design6): splitting 32:1 into 16:1→FF→2:1 cut ~260 ps.
- **Constant shift-add multipliers** replacing 32×32 signed multipliers (5 per pass ×2 passes): area 4274→~4100.
- **Width narrowing**: 20-bit signed transpose storage (row-pass outputs provably bounded by 2^19), 21-bit y-pass operand regs, 20-bit block_out — all provably lossless.

### 3. What didn't work / regressed
- Single 128-word transpose array → *longer* mux than 4 separate 32-word RAMs.
- 17-bit combining-stage registers (`o_t*`): far too narrow — constants carry ×4096 scaling so intermediates reach ~2^29; had to keep 32-bit wrap combining (correctness requires it).
- Truncating signed division `/256`: positive case has NO +1 bias (plain truncation); negative case is `(p+255)>>>8`.
- Verilator quirks: dynamic array-word select loses signedness (needs explicit sign-extension concat); mismatched port widths silently truncate (y-pass needed 20-bit ports, `IW_16(0)` param omission broke it).
- design8's dedicated per-constant multipliers: the original reloads operands at beats I missed (e.g. beat 2 re-mults `a0·C2`); my "simplification" of the beat→product mapping was wrong, and 3-stage product pipelines still left mismatches — ran out of steps.

### 4. Lessons
- **Don't simplify reference behavior by memory** — re-derive every scheduling detail (beat→operand reloads like `mul0_a <= block_in_0_1` with stale constant) from the RTL before restructuring; my costliest failures were from unverified assumptions about the 8-beat schedule.
- Failures come in *pairs* (a sign-extension loss + a wrong bias looked similar); diff actual vs. expected patterns (e.g. `0x000fff44` vs `0xffffff44`) pinpoints width/sign bugs fast.
- Mux structure dominates delay in register-array designs; algebraic access-pattern analysis (which words are ever read per cycle) beats generic "split the mux" fixes.
- A passing design7 exists; design8 (potentially ~15–25% area saving) is the natural next step: keep design7 wholesale, and port the dedicated-multiplier idea only after exhaustively tabulating the original's operand/constant loads per beat (including the redundant beat-2/4 loads).
